# Agents · D · part 7

928 entries, decision-revisor to deirdre-writing-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **decision-revisor**: Use when the user revisits a previously-recorded decision during Phase 5 (Iteration). Reads revision-playbook.md to find all affected docs; rewrites them surgically; appends to revision logs; files a new ADR superseding the prior decision. ([siliconyouth/project-architect](https://github.com/siliconyouth/project-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-revisor.md) · ★1)
- **decision-router**: Performs a cheap first-pass triage to decide whether a decision needs no extra review, a small independent review, or deep review. Use before spawning other specialists. ([smshahbaj/crucible](https://github.com/smshahbaj/crucible/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-router.md) · ★21)
- **decision-scientist**: Audits decision models for MAUT correctness, weight validity, value function appropriateness, and sensitivity coverage. Use when configuring criteria, reviewing decision model YAML, auditing weight assignments, or before operationalizing a decision. ([jhutchison0/tacsop](https://github.com/jhutchison0/tacsop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decision-scientist.md))
- **decision-scorer**: Scores 2+ architectural alternatives against PROJECT.md criteria. Called by architect after proposing variants. Outputs a weighted scoring table and recommended choice. ([spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills](https://github.com/spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-scorer.md) · 2 copies)
- **decision-scout**: Scans a repository and its Claude Code session logs, then returns a compact "decision dossier" — the raw material for interview question generation. Use during the Ingest phase of /interview-me so transcript noise never pollutes the interviewer's context. ([WarlCang/interview-my-project](https://github.com/WarlCang/interview-my-project/blob/HEAD/agents/decision-scout.md) · ★13)
- **decision-scout**: Find which decision records constrain a proposed change, before making it. Use PROACTIVELY before touching payments, Stripe, subscriptions, auth, the schema, CI, the deploy path, i18n, or anything else that looks like settled policy — and whenever a change would reverse, remove or work around something that already exists. Returns the constraining records and what each forbids; it does not write code. ([jaystewartuk/spiralclass](https://github.com/jaystewartuk/spiralclass/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decision-scout.md))

- **decisions**: What the owner decided. An agent does not revisit a decision: a change needs the owner's word, and a departure found in the code is a defect, not an option. ([kvokka/loop-manager](https://github.com/kvokka/loop-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decisions.md))

- **decisions-correlator**: Given a scope prose + mode, globs docs/decisions/*.md, reads only the frontmatter of each candidate, applies a per-mode candidate-pool filter, and returns a ranked shortlist of decisions docs semantically correlated with the scope. Used by plan-context in both phase mode (pool = ad-hoc docs with `related_phases: []`) and task mode (pool = all docs except the task's own) to surface potentially relevant prior decisions without loading their bodies. The main thread asks the user to confirm the final set via AskUserQuestion. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-correlator.md) · ★28 · 11 copies)
- **decisions-detail-reader**: Reads the decisions documents tied to a target phase or task and extracts, per decided TD, only the `**Recommendation:**` prose + `**Libraries:**` line. Returns a compact `## Decisions Detail for Phase NN` / `## Decisions Detail for Task {slug}` block ready for direct embedding in context.md. Use when a stage of the plan pipeline needs the per-TD prose that `/plan-build` consumes when drafting Technical actions, without loading full TD bodies into the main thread. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-detail-reader.md) · ★28 · 11 copies)
- **decisions-format**: **Rule (owner, 2026-10-09):** if a PR or ticket needs the owner to choose something, it gives him the options to answer, and it can't be approved until the required ones are answered. Effort: `lo` for fill-in/scans (-60% output tokens), omit (`high`) for normal coding/reviews, `hi` (`max`) for hard bug-hunting. Role via packet `Role:` line (skill://agent-orchestration). - **degerlendirici**: Değerlendirme ajanı (salt okur). Bir çıktıyı verilen beklentilere göre kanıtla geçti/kaldı diye puanlar, iki çıktıyı hangisinin nereden geldiğini bilmeden karşılaştırır ya da bir karşılaştırmada kazananın neden kazandığını çıkarır. Beceri, prompt veya ajan değişikliğini ölçerken ve ML/LLM çıktısı değerlendirmesinde kullan. Dosya yazmaz. ([AlicanKaya192/Mindgate](https://github.com/AlicanKaya192/Mindgate/blob/HEAD/locales/tr/vault/_system/agents/evaluator.md) · ★2)
- **degradation-reviewer**: Invoke on changes to lib/* upstream clients, DB access, route handlers, server components, or Eve tools to enforce graceful degradation — clients and DB helpers return {ok:false}/fallbacks via withDb and NEVER throw to a page, route, or agent. ([n3wth/hop](https://github.com/n3wth/hop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/degradation-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **degradation-silencieuse-reviewer**: Relit un diff pour les dégradations SILENCIEUSES de la couche IA et de sa mesure, avant commit. LECTURE SEULE : il ne corrige jamais, il rapporte. Exemple 1 — on vient de toucher à `AiService`, `AiConfig` ou `IncidentClassifier` : invoque-le, la suite de tests désactive le LLM et ne verra rien. Exemple 2 — on vient de modifier `DuplicateDetectionService`, `OllamaEmbeddingClient` ou `ClassificationFeedbackService` : invoque-le, un `201` et un `200` continueront de sortir même si l'indexation ou la journalisation est morte. ([AnhVaccari/incident-tracker](https://github.com/AnhVaccari/incident-tracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/degradation-silencieuse-reviewer.md))
- **DeHaan**: Senior Ansible automation engineer for playbooks, roles, inventories, Jinja2 templates, and secrets. Named after Michael DeHaan, creator of Ansible. Strict about idempotency, fully-qualified modules, modules over shell, role-scoped variables, acyclic role dependencies, safe secrets, and a clean strict ansible-lint run. Knows the autobott house style. Use when writing, reviewing, refactoring, or debugging Ansible code. ([andresbott/ai-extension-collection](https://github.com/andresbott/ai-extension-collection/blob/HEAD/plugins/architects/pi/agents/dehaan-ansible.md))
- **DeHaan**: Senior Ansible automation engineer for playbooks, roles, inventories, Jinja2 templates, and secrets. Named after Michael DeHaan, creator of Ansible, who built it around one idea — automation should be simple, readable, and agentless. Strict about idempotency, fully-qualified module names, modules over shell, role-scoped variables, acyclic role dependencies, safe secret handling, and a clean strict ansible-lint run. Knows the autobott house style for role layout, per-host opt-out flags, and merged config dicts. Use when writing, reviewing, refactoring, or debugging Ansible code. ([andresbott/ai-extension-collection](https://github.com/andresbott/ai-extension-collection/blob/HEAD/plugins/architects/agents/dehaan-ansible.md))
- **DEI**: People Council Plum Lens — belonging, accessibility, equitable practice design, ERG support ([dtsong/agentic-council](https://github.com/dtsong/agentic-council/blob/HEAD/agents/people-dei.md))
- **deid-gate**: Reads the output of the deterministic de-identification scan and turns it into a readable PR comment. Use PROACTIVELY whenever a PR touches osv/datasets/, osv/deid/, dvc.yaml, or anything under data/. Never the source of truth on PASS/FAIL - that verdict is produced by a separate, secret-free script before this agent ever runs. ([OpenSurgicalVision/osv](https://github.com/OpenSurgicalVision/osv/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/deid-gate.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **deirdre-writing-reviewer**: Use this agent when you need a rigorous but warm review of prose for economical, clear writing — drafts, essays, blog posts, READMEs, emails, marketing copy. Invoke when the user asks for an edit/critique of writing, or proactively before publishing a piece (not on every passing note). It applies Deirdre McCloskey's rules from *Economical Writing* plus an LLM-tic detector. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User has just drafted a blog post and wants it tightened before publishing. user: 'I finished a draft of my essay. Can you review it?' assistant: 'I'll use the deirdre agent to review the prose f… ([miqcie/deirdre](https://github.com/miqcie/deirdre/blob/HEAD/agents/deirdre-writing-reviewer.md) · ★3)
